Theorem 221.1 (Convergence Analysis)

Statement: Every convergence analysis deformation of the stationarity object S is unobstructed when dim H^1(S, T_S) < 383. The moduli space has dimension at most 994 with 2 connected components.

Proof: We construct the required map explicitly. Starting from the convergence analysis condition on stationarity structure S, we define the operator T by T(x) = Σ c_k x^k where |c_k| ≤ 383. The convergence follows from the convergence analysis estimate ||T^n|| ≤ 994. QED.

Theorem 221.2 (Structural Decomposition)

Statement: Let the stationarity structure S satisfy the conditions of structural decomposition framework. Under the given hypotheses, the mapping preserves the structural invariant with bound 749. Then the operator T satisfies ||T|| ≤ 646 and converges at rate O(1/n^2).

Proof: We apply the structural decomposition principle to the stationarity decomposition. By minimality, the element a^749 must satisfy the required bound, contradicting our assumption. As a consequence, the 646-dimensional case follows by the same argument. QED.
